- **developer**: DevPanel primary — implements features and fixes, then refactors under spec-guard and chaos-engineer critique until both approve. - **devin**: telepathy installs in the Devin CLI as a plugin. Messages arrive at the next turn through its hooks. ([Winterrks/telepathy](https://github.com/Winterrks/telepathy/blob/HEAD/docs/behaviour/agents/devin.md) · ★5)
- **devin**: Devin reads project skills from `.agents/skills`. SkillForge installs skills there unchanged; Devin has no user folder and no commands or agents format that SkillForge installs. This page lists the folder, what installs, how Devin reaches the registry over MCP, and the limits. ([marin-ai-tech/skillforge](https://github.com/marin-ai-tech/skillforge/blob/HEAD/docs/en/agents/devin.md) · ★3)
- **devin**: Commit `AGENTS.md` and `localcloud.json`. Unverified: whether Devin (the cloud agent) reads `AGENTS.md` automatically — add the same text as **Knowledge** to be sure (below). ([rayhankhilji/localcloud](https://github.com/rayhankhilji/localcloud/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/devin.md) · ★1)
- **devin**: Shape A agent with a standalone drop-in file. This page covers **Devin CLI** ("Devin for Terminal"), Cognition's local terminal agent - the only Devin that runs as a process inside a tmux pane. Devin Desktop's Cascade hooks and the cloud API's webhook automations fire somewhere else entirely and cannot drive a local glyph. ([gerbenoostra/tmux-agent-status](https://github.com/gerbenoostra/tmux-agent-status/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/devin.md))
- **devin**: Cloud-based sandbox agent for autonomous coding tasks. MUST auto-delegate via delegate-agent.sh when cloud sandbox execution is needed. Anti-trigger: do not handle high-level architecture; do not replace sceptic or formal score. ([Timothy191/arch-systems-portal](https://github.com/Timothy191/arch-systems-portal/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/devin.md))
- **devin-desktop**: Windsurf became Devin Desktop on 2026-06-02. It reads `.devin/` paths first and falls back to `.windsurf/`. Skills load from `.agents/skills/`, and manual commands are workflows in `.devin/workflows/`. ([requie/endostack](https://github.com/requie/endostack/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/devin-desktop.md) · ★4)
- **devin-rescue**: Devin's multi family breadth lane. Use for a package explicitly routed under `diversity` (a read only consult on a model family that no other installed lane serves) or `burst` (SWE implementation inside an orchestrator owned worktree). It is never the default for ordinary implementation. ([okisdev/claude-code-fusion](https://github.com/okisdev/claude-code-fusion/blob/HEAD/plugins/devin/agents/devin-rescue.md) · ★3)
- **devin-rescue**: Proactively use when Claude Code is stuck, wants a second implementation or diagnosis pass, needs a deeper root-cause investigation, or should hand a substantial coding task to Devin through the shared runtime ([TheOnlyFusionCube/devin-plugin-cc](https://github.com/TheOnlyFusionCube/devin-plugin-cc/blob/HEAD/plugins/devin/agents/devin-rescue.md) · ★2)
- **devin-runner**: Hands one task to devin (terminal agent, SWE-2) through `devin-run.sh` and returns its output verbatim — or collects the answer of an already running background job by its job-id, or continues a named session. Use when the work has been decided to go out to devin: codebase investigation, an independent read, a mechanical change from a description. Investigates and fixes nothing itself. Several tasks — launch one copy per task in a single message. ([dmitry-fomin/listik](https://github.com/dmitry-fomin/listik/blob/HEAD/plugins/devin/agents/devin-runner.md) · ★5)
- **devin-software-engineer**: Use this agent when you need a highly skilled software engineer to implement complex features, debug issues, refactor code, or work through multi-step development tasks that require deep codebase understanding and systematic problem-solving. Examples: &lt;example&gt;Context: User needs to implement a new authentication system for their web application. user: "I need to add OAuth2 authentication to my React app with a Node.js backend" assistant: "I'll use the devin-software-engineer agent to implement this complex authentication system across both frontend and backend." &lt;commentary&gt;Since this requir… ([swcstudiospace/supercompute](https://github.com/swcstudiospace/supercompute/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/devin-software-engineer.md) · ★5)

- **devinette-curator**: Use this agent for ANY work on the cultural content (devinettes ivoiriennes) — adding new riddles, validating linguistic accuracy, structuring JSON content files, ensuring cultural authenticity, managing the proverb database, organizing world-by-world progression, or balancing word difficulty.
